# Železné přísahy — detailed fictional chronology

## Method and cautions

This is structural restoration of the approved recovered Phase 2 chronology after finalized ingestion. Reviewed status preserves that prior approval; Step 4 awaits independent audit. Segment and thread numbering is navigation only, not a total temporal order.

The recovered reconstruction begins with a global posted_at/post_id scaffold and qualifies it from explicit continuations, elapsed time, retrospectives, delayed insertions, simultaneity, splits, and reunions. Narrative traversal never independently establishes fictional precedence.

Basis: Preserved recovery method and source interpretation constraints.

Confidence: high.

Sources: [Authorship and play authority; Cross-thread play and ordering; Curator-supplied corrections and chronology anchors](../../sources/campaigns/campaign-zelezne-prisahy/source-rpgforum-340.md).

- posted_at order is a default scaffold, not fictional chronology
- posting username does not mechanically establish the authorial role of MarkyParky or Jezus posts
- real-world authors behind Orákulum posts were not investigated
- participants and locations remain unresolved source labels (historical Phase 2 status; existing finalized identities are linked only where correspondence was already established).
- the source value Bjorn in topic 17686, post 689891 is interpreted as Tristan per curator/GM correction and is not Bjorn-presence evidence

Basis: Preserved source interpretation cautions.

Confidence: high.

Sources: [Authorship and play authority; Cross-thread play and ordering; Curator-supplied corrections and chronology anchors](../../sources/campaigns/campaign-zelezne-prisahy/source-rpgforum-340.md).

## Anchors and corrections

### Opening year — chronology_anchor_001

Campaign opening in 1032 poPA, at year precision only.

Basis: GM-supplied fictional chronology anchor. Year precision only; no month, day, season boundary, or 1032-01-01 is implied. The year is not propagated to later segments without supporting relative chronology.

### chronology_review_001

Post 689891 contains a character-name error: interpret Bjorn as Tristan. The post supplies no evidence of Bjorn's return or presence in Bouřný Les, so the apparent segment_039/segment_040 conflict is removed.

Basis: curator/GM clarification supplied on 2026-08-22

Confidence: high.

Sources: [topic 17686, post 689891](../../sources/campaigns/campaign-zelezne-prisahy/rpgforum-340-normalized/topics/17686.md#post-689891); [section 'Curator-supplied corrections and chronology anchors'](../../sources/campaigns/campaign-zelezne-prisahy/source-rpgforum-340.md#curator-supplied-corrections-and-chronology-anchors).

## Interpretation limits

The recovered phase-3 notes below are historical analytical scope cautions, not a new unresolved chronology queue. Current finalized identities and the existing curation queue remain authoritative for identity resolution. No new identity resolution is performed here.

- `uncertainty_006` / historical `unresolved_for_phase_3`: Semantic scope of Svatyně, Rattle, Tábor, and Strážci was not resolved.
- `uncertainty_007` / historical `unresolved_for_phase_3`: Potentially distinct people with reused/common names were not merged.
- `uncertainty_008` / historical `unresolved_for_phase_3`: State-changing referents were not split or merged.

Basis: Preserved conservative identity scope; no new equivalence or fictional fact asserted.

Confidence: high.

Sources: [Authorship and play authority; Cross-thread play and ordering; Curator-supplied corrections and chronology anchors](../../sources/campaigns/campaign-zelezne-prisahy/source-rpgforum-340.md).

Basis: Preserved curator resolution.

Confidence: high.
